Creates self as LUI.UIElement.new, Background as LUI.UIImage.new, NoiseBacking as LUI.UIImage.new, TiledShaderImage as LUI.UIImage.new, CommonCornerPips as CoD.CommonCornerPips01.new, SpawnSelectIconBg as CoD.SpawnSelectLocationMarker.new, SpawnSelectPulse as CoD.SpawnRegionPulse.new, AutoSpawnPoint as CoD.SpawnRegionAutoSpawn.new, Label as LUI.UIText.new. Stores source state predicates in order Hidden. Requests model links self, "visStatus", true. Owns 2 nested callable scopes, each with its separate delivery/invocation contract. Registers LUI.OverrideFunction_CallOriginalSecond( self, "close", self.__onClose ). Runs PostLoadFunc( self, f1_arg1, f1_arg0 ) only under its exact guard and at its original source position. - `hmh07-lua-follow1:088` (original lines 5-10, 5-10): Builds game-status decorative panels, kills/players-left children, teammate/spectator counts and left/right endgame status lists. Nonnil aliveTeammateCount/spectatorsCount are sent directly to text. Each status display filters right-solo gameInformation versus left-solo hudGameStat, then assigns its exact endgame datasource names. Registers close, optional postload, returns self. This displays supplied data without computing ranks, population or results.

- `hmh07-lua:191` (original lines 5-5, 5-10): Fill-squad outer constructor directly creates this internal branch and changes its status/labels afterward. Builds blur/tint/focus/disabled layers, normal/focus off-status and text. Disabled = not WZUtility.IsFillSquadAvailable(controller), reevaluated on global playlistId or lobbyList.playerCount changes. This child does not install a toggle button or write the party-fill setting. Qualified reset and DefaultState/Disabled focus clips control brightness/brackets/status text, register transition/interrupted handlers and loop nextClip=Focus where specified. Linked __onClose closes FocusBrackets then DotTiledBacking; CallOriginalSecond close hook, optional postload then self return. Exact squad availability/native setting and keyframe completion remain conditional.

- `hmh07-lua:179` (original lines 5-5, 5-10): Common lobby creates MapVotePC directly, alongside a separate MapVoteZM; a PC-looking field name is not a construction gate. Builds decided/random/previous/next entries. Model subscriptions update map images, names, mode names/icons and counts only for nonnil values; lobbyStatus text is uppercased. Confirm on random/next calls LobbyMapVoteSelectRandom/Next and returns true. Previous confirm and label require MapVotePreviousSelectable; disallowed confirm falls through without success and label returns false. Chosen child events play the corresponding parent clip then dispatch to children; focus handlers try local or super gainFocus, refresh confirm label and return its result. States are VOTING->MapVote, LOCKEDIN plus exact vote enum->ChosenNext/Previous/Random, and LOCKEDIN->SelectedMap. MapVote/lobbyNav/voteType notifications only schedule state reevaluation, not choose a vote. The setState CallOriginalFirst hook conditionally arranges SelectedMap on clip-over. Constructor returns self, close uses self.__onClose through CallOriginalSecond. Frozen class clip/reset/close companions are source-linked, with per-state transactions retained below; actual enum priority, backend vote delivery, callbacks, animation completion/cancellation and effective HMH visibility remain external.

Conditional constructor creates menu, setups default-focus callbacks/any-controller/offline-screen/build-info/companion-app/contracts model, then builds theater/custom/training/arena/public/private/pregame/select and shared/PC-looking children. Direct construction is not authority to reconstruct every other-mode child. Child menu_loaded wrappers use local/super menuLoaded, conditional non-PC safe-area sizing, then dispatch only if unhandled. State predicates: HideForGameStart from LobbyRoot hideMenusForGameStart1; ShowSelectKorea15Plus requires PC+Zombies+opaque Korea eligibility; other states delegate exact DirectorUtility ShowDirectorSelect/Pregame/Private/Custom/Arena/ArenaMatchmaking/Theater/Public/Training. lobbyNav/showSelect/hide flags reevaluate state; network/client/input/xuid/time/spinner/stage/session changes refresh button state. unused_gamepad_button invokes LobbyAddLocalClient(event.controller or owner) then dispatches children. Non-PC Start opens StartMenu_Main; non-PC social requires online/nonLAN/nonguest/allowed/not-demo-disabled/not-training, while PC select Triangle activates featured. Square branches offline-demo LAN hold behavior or nonPC select featured activation. Escape/mouse-keyboard and Circle/gamepad have separate ordered chains: hide preview, clear selected client, cancel/leave applicable custom/arena/theater/public states subject to spinner/timer/host/eligibility, or PC select quit. Duplicate preview branches are preserved and may be shadowed by earlier identical predicates; no guessed simplification. Back opens AAR only when CanShowAAR and refreshes prompt. RTrigger gestures requires dvar lobby_gestures_enabled plus opaque hash_61E20A632CB63092; then alias/preserve-button/open selector. setState CallOriginalFirst hook conditionally opens AAR/refreshes contract/client updates or initializes training; external predicates keep non-HMH internals conditional. Close CallOriginalFirst hook clears saved state and OnDirectorClose; class __onClose registered via CallOriginalSecond outside selected body remains dependency. lobby room/isMatchmaking callbacks refresh room; hideMenusForGameStart1 plays hide clip+loses focus/hides cursor,0 plays default/unhides cursor. menu_loaded then optional restore/postload; PC branch additionally key-color/friends/Korea/navigation/ZM-availability hooks, console branch DirectorUtility.PostLoad only. Exact default-focus registrations occur at end, then self returned. No live lobby/account action was executed; backend/model/enum/native dispatch, actual visibility and clip/close bodies remain open.
